|---|---|---|
| f3_i64_add_sub | c | pair: the unit's own ledger attests no single (setter, consumer) pair; body: the dictionary has no proved emulation of the pool entry E00029 on c: the emulation study's filter P1 keeps only an entry the target has NO member of, and this entry has 166 member(s) in c; cell: the emulation `emu_add_gpr_gpr_64__primitive__c` declares its parameter 0 in c's truth holder `bool`, which collapses every non-zero value to one; the proof was made over that holder and this node's operand is not in it |
| f6_i32_div | c | `a / b`: the corpus names this node the unit go/op_96, and the dictionary serves it at no level -- cell: go's own build of this construct is `push %rbp; mov %rsp,%rbp; test %ebx,%ebx; je 47a658 <main.op_96+0x18>; cmp $0xffffffff,%ebx; jne 47a653 <main.op_96+0x13>; neg %eax; xor %edx,%edx; jmp 47a656 <main.op_96+0x16>; cltd; idiv %ebx; pop %rbp; ret; call 43f3c0 <runtime.panicdivide>; nop`, which is not one arch-opcode instruction plus chaff (task o2's narrow rule), so the corpus attests no single cell for it; its own ledger holds 8 arch-opcode row(s): `push gpr_one 64`, `test gpr_same 32`, `cmp imm_gpr 32`, `neg gpr_one 32`, `xor gpr_same 32`, `cltd none 64`, `idiv gpr_one 32`, `idiv gpr_one 32`; pair: the unit's own ledger attests no single (setter, consumer) pair; body: the dictionary has no proved emulation of the pool entry E00302 on c: the emulation study's filter P2 keeps only an entry that carries a layer-5 text, and this entry carries none |
| f8_i32_select | c | `a != b`: the corpus names this node the unit go/op_492, and the dictionary serves it at no level -- cell: go's own build of this construct is `cmp %eax,%ebx; setne %al; ret`, which is not one arch-opcode instruction plus chaff (task o2's narrow rule), so the corpus attests no single cell for it; its own ledger holds 1 arch-opcode row(s): `cmp gpr_gpr 32`; pair: the dictionary has no proved entry for the pair `cmp gpr_gpr 32 + setne gpr_one 8` on c: the loop renders each consumer over ONE setter -- the one that consumer's own attestation records the most ledger rows for -- and for this consumer that setter is `test gpr_gpr 8`, which is not the setter this pair is over; body: the dictionary has no proved emulation of the pool entry E00163 on c: the emulation study's filter P1 keeps only an entry the target has NO member of, and this entry has 429 member(s) in c |
